</div><h2 class="slogan">Publish early,<br/>publish often</h2></div></div><h4 align="left"><strong>Earn 80% royalties publishing on Leanpub</strong></h4><p align="left">Authors have earned over <strong>$8,000,000</strong> publishing ebooks and courses on Leanpub. You can use Leanpub to <strong>write, publish and sell</strong> an ebook or online course as well.</p><p align="left">You can write your book in our <strong>visual editor</strong> or in a plain text document formatted in <strong>Markdown</strong>. Either way, you can publish it as a Leanpub ebook with <strong>one click</strong>. You can publish your book while you're writing it, or wait until it is done: it's up to you.</p><p align="left">Or, if you've already written a book, you can <strong>upload a completed ebook</strong> to sell in our store.</p><p align="left">You can <strong>create a Leanpub course in Markdown</strong> as well. Leanpub courses are like Leanpub books, but learners read them and do the quizzes and exercises on the Leanpub website.</p></div><div class="create-book-actions"><a class="btn--solid" href="/create/book">Create a Book or Course</a><a class="btn--hollow" href="/authors">Learn More</a></div><p></p></aside><div class="featured-book-cover-wrapper"><div class="book-cover hero"><a href="/effectivekotlin"><img alt="Effective Kotlin" src="https://d2sofvawe08yqg.cloudfront.net/effectivekotlin/hero?1565003966" style="width: auto;"/></a><div class="book-progress"><div class="progress-bar" title="This book is 100% complete."><span class="progress-bar__meter" style="width: 100%;"></span></div><p class="percent-complete"><span>This book is 100% complete</span></p><p class="last-updated"><span>Completed on 2019-10-12</span></p></div></div></div><div class="featured-book-summary"><p class="featured-title">Featured Book</p><a href="/effectivekotlin"><h3 class="featured-book-title ltr">Effective Kotlin</h3></a><h5 class="featured-book-subtitle">Best practices</h5><div class="featured-book-author"><a class="avatar-with-name--horizontal link" href="/u/mmoskala"><div class="avatar--tiny"><img alt="Marcin Moskala" class="avatar__image" src="https://d39qdlcrvnra4b.cloudfront.net/avatars/1251350/tiny/Marcin.jpg?1523901718"/></div><span class="avatar-with-name__name">Marcin Moskala</span></a></div><div class="featured-book-tagline"><p>Effective Kotlin summarizes the best practices and experiences of the Kotlin community, together with a deep explanation of some lesser-known Kotlin functionalities.         document.querySelector('.cookies-banner').remove()

      })

<script charset="UTF-8" data-reactroot="">window.__data={"AdminFlag":{"all":{"1":{"type":"AdminFlag","flag":false,"slug":"homepage_essay","subtitle":null,"text":"\u003Ch2 id=\"leanpub-and-climate-change\"\u003ELeanpub and Climate Change\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ELeanpub launched on April 27, 2010. It’s now September 25, 2019, so we’ve been working hard on Leanpub for over 9 years!\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EOver these years, Leanpub authors have done many amazing things. You’ve reached millions of readers and earned over $8 million USD in royalties. (Leanpub pays 80% royalties to authors.)\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EHowever, there’s one other thing that Leanpub authors and readers have done together which I doubt any of them know:\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EYou’ve helped save up to 25 million pounds of CO2.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Ch3 id=\"the-math\"\u003EThe Math\u003C\u002Fh3\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EPrint books may look and smell nice, but this comes at a huge cost. According to \u003Ca href=\"http:\u002F\u002Fwww.greenpointpublishing.com\u002Finsight_html\u002Freason-love-Ebooks.html\"\u003EGreenPoint\u003C\u002Fa\u003E, “on average, each printed book releases 8.85 pounds of carbon dioxide into the environment. Together, the newspaper and book-printing industries cut down 125 million trees per year and emit 44 million tons of CO2.”\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ENow, from when Leanpub launched to the time of writing, we’ve had 2,875,093 free and paid ebook purchases. Of these purchases, about 20% (574,166) have been paid, and about 80% (2,300,927) have been free. Some of these purchases have been for multiple copies, but for the purposes of this back-of-the-envelope math, every free and paid purchase will count as saving one printed book. (Leanpub has only ever sold ebooks.)\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ESince each printed book releases about 8.85 pounds of CO2, this means that readers buying Leanpub ebooks instead of print books has saved a lot of CO2:\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cul\u003E\n  \u003Cli\u003EIf you count all free and paid purchases, you’ve helped save up to 25,444,573 pounds of CO2. This is 11,541,478 kilograms of CO2, or 11,541 metric tonnes of CO2.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\n  \u003Cli\u003EIf you count only paid purchases, you’ve helped save up to 5,081,369 pounds of CO2. This is 2,304,873 kilograms of CO2, or 2,304 metric tonnes of CO2.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\n\u003C\u002Ful\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EGiven the \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.youtube.com\u002Fwatch?v=KAJsdgTPJpU\"\u003Eclimate emergency\u003C\u002Fa\u003E we are in, the CO2 saved is the important thing.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EThese savings compound, by the way. Reading ebooks saves trees, and the trees which are used to print books would otherwise be busy reducing atmospheric CO2, so they’re a good thing to save. Specifically, since one tree can produce an \u003Ca href=\"http:\u002F\u002Fepublishersweekly.blogspot.com\u002F2009\u002F09\u002Febooks-save-millions-of-trees-10-ideas.html\"\u003Eestimated\u003C\u002Fa\u003E 62.5 books, you’ve saved a lot of trees:\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cul\u003E\n  \u003Cli\u003EIf you count all free and paid purchases, you’ve helped save up to 46,001 trees.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\n  \u003Cli\u003EIf you count only paid purchases, you’ve helped save up to 9,187 trees.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\n\u003C\u002Ful\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EYes, even though some computer programming books are printed on partially or fully recycled paper, this still counts. Using no paper at all is a lot better than using recycled paper, since recycled paper is fungible: if the recycled paper was not being used to produce physical books, it could be used for something else where there is no effective substitute. You know, like toilet paper.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EAlso, producing, shipping and storing physical books still produces CO2, even if those books are printed on recycled paper. And don’t get me started on all the waste in storing, shipping and destroying unsold physical books.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ENow, Leanpub is a tiny company. So, while we’ve had a small impact, so much more could be done.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Ch3 id=\"stop-the-presses\"\u003EStop the Presses\u003C\u002Fh3\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ELeanpub primarily sells computer programming books and courses.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EMost physical computer programming books have a short shelf life: after about a year they will be outdated, and after about two years most will be obsolete. My first traditionally-published print book was outdated in a couple years, but the second one \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fleanpub.com\u002Flean\u002Fread#leanpub-auto-introduction\"\u003Ebecame obsolete a lot faster\u003C\u002Fa\u003E.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EThis is not true of ebooks, however. An ebook can be kept current for as long as the author chooses to do so. At Leanpub, we make this as easy as possible: you can update your manuscript in plain text, and then publish a new version with one click. You get to choose whether to notify your existing readers of the new version, etc. We call our publishing process \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fleanpub.com\u002Flean\u002Fread#leanpub-auto-definition\"\u003ELean Publishing\u003C\u002Fa\u003E, and it’s why Leanpub is called Leanpub. Other publishers who sell direct to their readers can also produce and distribute ebook updates in a similar, if more cumbersome, manner.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ENow, some computer programming books are exceptions and are relevant for a lot longer. For example, a new edition of \u003Cem\u003E\u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fpragprog.com\u002Fbook\u002Ftpp20\u002Fthe-pragmatic-programmer-20th-anniversary-edition\"\u003EThe Pragmatic Programmer\u003C\u002Fa\u003E\u003C\u002Fem\u003E was just released, as it still has something to say after 20 years. However, the reason this is true is that it’s about general principles, not about a specific programming language or technology stack. Furthermore, even \u003Cem\u003EThe Pragmatic Programmer\u003C\u002Fem\u003E can be read perfectly well as an ebook, and we are in a climate emergency.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ETwo Leanpub competitors who we greatly respect are \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.manning.com\u002F\"\u003EManning\u003C\u002Fa\u003E and \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fpragprog.com\u002F\"\u003EThe Pragmatic Programmers\u003C\u002Fa\u003E. When I was young, I learned to program from their books (and O’Reilly books), and I’ve even written a couple of Manning books myself.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ESomeone in the software industry in 2019 could limit their book purchasing to ebooks from Leanpub, Manning and The Pragmatic Programmers and they’d have most of their professional book needs met.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ESo, I’m personally calling on Manning and The Pragmatic Programmers to completely stop producing physical books by the end of 2019.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EStop the presses.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EIf you do this, you will sell more ebooks. Since your ebooks have a higher margin than print books, you may make more profit–even while holding costs constant and putting in the same editorial, layout and marketing work to your books that you do today. Furthermore, since you sell ebooks direct, you will make most of the money, not Amazon.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EJust like Leanpub, both Manning and The Pragmatic Programmers sell their ebooks direct from their websites. Chances are many Leanpub readers have, like me, bought a number of ebooks directly from both of them.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EHardly any readers who want to buy a particular book from Leanpub, Manning or The Pragmatic Programmers will think “gee, this book which I want doesn’t exist in print book form, I guess I’ll go buy a different book from some other publisher”.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EI wouldn’t do this, and neither would you. Books are nowhere near as fungible as paper: some books are better than others, and you should buy and read the best ones.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003ELeanpub, Manning and The Pragmatic Programmers make some of the best computer programming books on the market. Let’s send a signal to the market that these books are even better because they didn’t generate thousands of tonnes of CO2 and kill thousands of trees in the process.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EFurthermore, if Leanpub, Manning and The Pragmatic Programmers all don’t sell physical books, maybe we can start a movement in the computer programming book industry in particular, and in the larger industry overall.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Ch3 id=\"gratitude-and-goals\"\u003EGratitude and Goals\u003C\u002Fh3\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EI recently took a proper vacation: it started with a family vacation, and I added a solo hiking vacation onto the end of it.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EWhen I was totally alone in the mountains, I realized just how not alone I was. I wasn’t just connected to my family back in Victoria, but to my team at Leanpub, and to all the Leanpub authors and readers all over the world.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EI recently turned 44. So, I’ve probably lived about half my life.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EIt is a good life. I have a wonderful family whom I love very much, I live in a truly beautiful city, and I get to wake up every day and work on something which I believe in.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EThis isn’t a cynical belief, like “I believe the market will buy at this valuation.” It’s a deep belief in Leanpub’s mission.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EJust continuing along the path we’re on, sometime in 2020 or 2021 Leanpub should hit $10 million USD in royalties paid to authors. At that point, we’ll have saved up to 30 million pounds of CO2, and up to thousands more trees.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EHowever, there’s a lot more that could be done. If we can scale Leanpub 10x we can pay authors about $100 million USD in royalties and save hundreds of millions of pounds of CO2, and if we can scale Leanpub 100x we can pay authors about $1 billion USD in royalties while saving billions of pounds of CO2.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EThese are important goals, and for me, pursuing important goals is the right way to live. I am deeply grateful to every Leanpub author and reader for giving me this opportunity.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\n\u003Cp\u003EPeter Armstrong\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E\nFounder &amp; CEO, Leanpub\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E\nSeptember 25, 2019\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n","title":null,"updated_at":"2019-09-26T17:19:19.000Z","id":1},"2":{"type":"AdminFlag","flag":false,"slug":"homepage_news","subtitle":"Our new support for fullbleed images will especially help travel guidebooks and cookbooks...","text":"We recently \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fmedium.com\u002F@leanpub\u002Ffullbleed-images-and-the-height-attribute-now-work-in-markua-mode-on-leanpub-a51b1e4120a6\"\u003Eadded support for fullbleed images to Leanpub\u003C\u002Fa\u003E. reliable water services requires not only a sufficient volume of water but also significant amounts of data. Water professionals continuously measure the flow and quality of the water and how customers perceive their service. Data and water are natural partners. Water utilities are awash, or even flooded with data. Data professionals use data pipelines and data lakes and cause data to flow from one place to another.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThe term digital water utility has become a popular buzzword in the industry. A digital utility can only exist when the people that manage the water improve their skills. This workshop introduces participants to analysing data using the \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.r-project.org\u002F\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ER language\u003C\u002Fa\u003E for statistical computing. \u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThis course does not provide not an exhaustive introduction into data science programming but merely a teaser to inspire water professionals to ditch their spreadsheets and start writing code to create value from data.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThe content of this course is available free of charge for any employee of organisations who are members of \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.waterra.com.au\u002F\" target=\"_blank\"\u003EWater Research Australia\u003C\u002Fa\u003E, Intelligent Water Networks (\u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.iwn.org.au\u002F\" target=\"_blank\"\u003EIWN\u003C\u002Fa\u003E) or the Smart Water Networks forum (\u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.swan-forum.com\u002F\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ESWAN\u003C\u002Fa\u003E). Contact your representative to get free access.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThis course opens with an introduction to the principles of data science and the R language. The following three sessions consist of realistic case studies where participants solve water management problems using R code.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Cp\u003E \u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003EPrinciples of Water Utility Data Science\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThis course is not only about the vocabulary and syntax of R, but also about producing good data science. This session introduces a framework for best practice in analysing data and sharing the results. This framework derives from the book \u003Ca href=\"https:\u002F\u002Fwww.packtpub.com\u002Fbig-data-and-business-intelligence\u002Fprinciples-strategic-data-science\" target=\"_blank\"\u003EPrinciples of Strategic Data Science\u003C\u002Fa\u003E. The three case studies each implement aspects of this framework.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003EIntroduction to the R Language\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThe second session introduces the basic principles of the R language and applies these principles to measuring the flow in an open channel.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003ECase Study 1: Water Quality Regulations\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EIn this first case study, participants apply their skills to laboratory testing data from an imaginary drinking water network. The case study revolves around checking the data for compliance with water quality regulations.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003EThe Tidyverse\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThe Tidyverse is an extension of the R language that provides additional functionality to simplify manipulating, analysing and presenting data science. The fourth session delves into the basic principles of visualising data with the ggpot2 library, using data from the first case study.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003ECase Study 2: Understanding Customer Perception\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThe data for the second case study consists of the results of a survey of American consumers about their perception of tap water services. Participants use the Tidyverse to clean, transform and visualise this data.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003EData Products\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EThe fifth session focuses on the data science workflow as an iterative process to solve a data problem. This session also introduces R Markdown as a tool to report on the results of a data science project. In this session, students prepare a report to summarise the impact of proposed changes to water regulations on the data in the first case study.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\u003Ch2\u003ECase Study 3: Analysing Water Consumption\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\u003Cp\u003EIn the last case study, participants use the analytical functionalities of the Tidyverse to analyse data from smart meters to find anomalies in water consumption.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E","attempts_allowed":1,"clicky_analytics_code":null,"copyright_html":"\u003Cdiv class=\"cc-license\"\u003E\n\u003Ca href=\"http:\u002F\u002Fcreativecommons.org\u002Flicenses\u002Fby-sa\u002F4.0\u002Fdeed.en_US\"\u003E\u003Cimg src=\"\u002Fcc_images\u002Fcc-by-sa.png\" alt=\"Creative Commons by-sa\" \u002F\u003E\u003C\u002Fa\u003E\n\u003Cbr \u002F\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThis work is licensed under a \u003Ca href=\"http:\u002F\u002Fcreativecommons.org\u002Flicenses\u002Fby-sa\u002F4.0\u002Fdeed.en_US\"\u003ECreative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 4.0 International License\u003C\u002Fa\u003E\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\n\u003C\u002Fdiv\u003E\n","copyright_in_footer":true,"created_at":"2019-06-03T08:05:49.000Z","current_published_version_id":2030,"discourse_group_exists":true,"discourse_group_url":"https:\u002F\u002Fcommunity.leanpub.com\u002Fc\u002Fr4h2o","discourse_slug":"r4h2o","enabled_forums":true,"forum_url":"https:\u002F\u002Fcommunity.leanpub.com\u002Fc\u002Fr4h2o","full_slug":"courses\u002Fleanpub\u002FR4H2O","google_analytics_code":null,"home_page_path":"\u002Fcourses\u002Fleanpub\u002FR4H2O\u002Fhome","instructors_label":"Instructor","landing_page_path_or_url":"\u002Fcourses\u002Fleanpub\u002FR4H2O","material_book_id":54740,"meta_description":"The digital water utility requires professionals to embrace the latest developments in using data.
